    SELECT j.guid, j."Description", j."EmploymentPosition", j."EmploymentType", j."IsDeleted", j."LastDate", j."Maxsalary", j."Minsalary", j."PostedDate", j."RecruiterID", j."Title", r.guid AS guid0, r."CompanyId", r."Designation", r."FirstName", r."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", r."LastName", r."PhoneNumber", r."UserId", c.guid AS guid1, c."CompanyName", c."Description" AS "Description0", c."IndustryTypeId", c."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted1", c."WebsiteUrl"
    FROM "jobPosts" AS j
    INNER JOIN recruiters AS r ON j."RecruiterID" = r.guid
    INNER JOIN companies AS c ON r."CompanyId" = c.guid
    WHERE NOT (j."IsDeleted") AND j.guid = @__key_0
    LIMIT 1
) AS s3
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT p.guid, p."JobPostId", p."SkillId", s.guid AS guid0, s."Name"
    FROM "postSkills" AS p
    INNER JOIN skills AS s ON p."SkillId" = s.guid
) AS s4 ON s3.guid = s4."JobPostId"
LEFT JOIN responsibilities AS r0 ON s3.guid = r0."PostId"
LEFT JOIN locations AS l ON s3.guid = l."JobPostId"
LEFT JOIN requirements AS r1 ON s3.guid = r1."PostId"

    FROM "jobApplications" AS j0
    INNER JOIN seekers AS s0 ON j0."SeekerId" = s0.guid
    LEFT JOIN educations AS e ON s0.guid = e."SeekerId"
    LEFT JOIN experiences AS e0 ON s0.guid = e0."SeekerId"
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT s1.guid, s1."SeekerId", s1."SkillId", s2.guid AS guid0, s2."Name"
        FROM "seekerSkills" AS s1
        INNER JOIN skills AS s2 ON s1."SkillId" = s2.guid
    ) AS s5 ON s0.guid = s5."SeekerId"
) AS s6 ON s3.guid = s6."JobPostId"
